\leq{}&\int_{s}^{t}\Big\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{J}}(D^{2}\Pi)(X_{\kappa(u),u})\Big(B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\tilde{e}_{i},B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\tilde{e}_{i}\Big)\Big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H)}\,\mathrm{d}u\\
\leq{}&c_{1}\int_{s}^{t}\Big\|\|X_{\kappa(u),u}\|_{H}\|B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}\Big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\,\mathrm{d}u\\
\leq{}&c_{1}\eta^{2}\int_{s}^{t}\|X_{\kappa(u),u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H)}\,\mathrm{d}u\leq{}c_{1}\eta^{3}\,p\int_{s}^{t}(u-\kappa(u))^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\,\mathrm{d}u\leq{}c_{1}\eta^{3}\,p(t-s)^{1-\alpha}|\theta|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}T^{\alpha}.\end{split}

(182)

Combining
(
46
),
(
179
),
(
180
),
(
177
),
(
181
),
(
182
), and
(
172
)
proves
for all

and all

that

This finishes the proof of Lemma

.
∎

The next lemma improves the regularity of the moment estimates.

Lemma 3.12
.

Assume Setting

, and all

it holds that

assume that for all

,
x\in H
, and all

\displaystyle\|F(t,x)\|_{H_{\alpha_{1}}}\leq c_{2}(\|x\|_{H_{\alpha_{2}}}^{q_{2}}+1),

(189)

\displaystyle\|\Pi(x)\|_{H}\leq c_{1}\|x\|_{H},

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{J}}(D^{2}\Pi)(x)\big(z\tilde{e}_{i},z\tilde{e}_{i}\big)\Big\|_{H}\leq c_{1}\|x\|_{H}\cdot\|z\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)},

(193)

and assume that

\Big\|(D\Pi)(X_{\kappa(u),u})(AX_{\llcorner u\lrcorner_{\theta},u})-A\Pi(X_{\kappa(u),u})\Big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H)}\leq c_{1}.

(194)

Then it holds for all

that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|Y_{t}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\gamma})}\leq{}&\|Y_{0}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\gamma})}+\tfrac{c_{2}}{1-(\gamma-\alpha_{1})}(\tfrac{\gamma-\alpha_{1}}{e})^{\gamma-\alpha_{1}}t^{1-(\gamma-\alpha_{1})}(1+\sup_{u\in\theta}\|Y_{\llcorner u\lrcorner_{\theta}}\|^{q_{2}}_{L^{pq_{2}}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\alpha_{2}})})\\
&+c_{1}+\tfrac{c_{1}\eta^{3}p}{1-\gamma}\,(\tfrac{\gamma}{e})^{\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}|\theta|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\\
&+2\sqrt{p}\min\Big\{t^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}-\gamma}\tfrac{(c_{1}\eta\,p\,|\theta|^{1/2}+1)\eta}{\sqrt{1-2\gamma}}(\tfrac{\gamma}{e})^{\gamma},c_{3}(\inf_{i\in\mathcal{I}}|\lambda_{i}|)^{\gamma-\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\eta+l(\kappa)\\
&\qquad+(\tfrac{\gamma-\gamma_{2}}{e})^{\gamma-\gamma_{2}}c\eta^{2}\,p(\tfrac{\gamma_{2}}{e})^{\gamma_{2}}t^{1-\gamma}\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{1-2\gamma_{2}}}\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{1+2\gamma_{2}-2\gamma}}\Big\}.\end{split}

(195)

Proof.

We estimate the terms on the right-hand side of (

).
First note that

prove for all

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Big\|\int^{t}_{0}e^{(t-u)A}\mathbbm{1}_{D}(Y_{\kappa(u)})F(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\,\mathrm{d}u\Big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\gamma})}\\
\leq{}&\int^{t}_{0}\|e^{(t-u)A}\mathbbm{1}_{D}(Y_{\kappa(u)})F(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\gamma})}\,\mathrm{d}u\\
\leq{}&\int^{t}_{0}\big\|\|e^{(t-u)A}(-A)^{\gamma-\alpha_{1}}\|_{L(H,H)}\|(-A)^{\alpha_{1}}F(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\|_{H}\big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\,\mathrm{d}u\\
\leq{}&c_{2}\int^{t}_{0}\big\|\big(\sup_{\lambda\in(0,\infty)}\lambda^{\gamma-\alpha_{1}}e^{-(t-u)\lambda}\big)(1+\|Y_{\kappa(u)}\|^{q_{2}}_{H_{\alpha_{2}}})\big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\,\mathrm{d}u\\
\leq{}&c_{2}\int^{t}_{0}(\tfrac{\gamma-\alpha_{1}}{e})^{\gamma-\alpha_{1}}(t-u)^{-(\gamma-\alpha_{1})}(1+\|Y_{\kappa(u)}\|^{q_{2}}_{L^{pq_{2}}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\alpha_{2}})})\,\mathrm{d}u\\
\leq{}&\tfrac{c_{2}}{1-(\gamma-\alpha_{1})}(\tfrac{\gamma-\alpha_{1}}{e})^{\gamma-\alpha_{1}}t^{1-(\gamma-\alpha_{1})}(1+\sup_{u\in[0,T]}\|Y_{\kappa(u)}\|^{q_{2}}_{L^{pq_{2}}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\alpha_{2}})}).\end{split}

(196)

In addition,

\forall\lambda\in(0,\infty)\colon\lambda^{\gamma}e^{-\lambda}\leq(\tfrac{\gamma}{e})^{\gamma}
,
(

),
(
193
),

3.3

(with

and with

)

t\in[0,T]
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Big\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{(t-u)A}\mathbbm{1}_{D}(Y_{\kappa(u)})\sum_{i\in\mathcal{J}}(D^{2}\Pi)(X_{\kappa(u),u})\Big(B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\tilde{e}_{i},B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\tilde{e}_{i}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}u\Big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\gamma})}\\
\leq{}&\int_{0}^{t}\Big\|e^{(t-u)A}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{J}}(D^{2}\Pi)(X_{\kappa(u),u})\Big(B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\tilde{e}_{i},B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\tilde{e}_{i}\Big)\Big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\gamma})}\,\mathrm{d}u\\
\leq{}&\int_{0}^{t}\big\|e^{(t-u)A}(-A)^{\gamma}\big\|_{L(H,H)}\Big\|\sum_{i\in\mathcal{J}}(D^{2}\Pi)(X_{\kappa(u),u})\Big(B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\tilde{e}_{i},B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\tilde{e}_{i}\Big)\Big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H)}\,\mathrm{d}u\\
\leq{}&c_{1}\int_{0}^{t}\big(\sup_{\lambda\in(0,\infty)}e^{(t-u)\lambda}\lambda^{\gamma}\big)\big\|\|X_{\kappa(u),u}\|_{H}\|B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}\big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\,\mathrm{d}u\\
\leq{}&c_{1}\eta^{2}\int_{0}^{t}(\tfrac{\gamma}{e})^{\gamma}(t-u)^{-\gamma}\|X_{\kappa(u),u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H)}\,\mathrm{d}u\leq{}\tfrac{c_{1}\eta^{3}p}{1-\gamma}\,(\tfrac{\gamma}{e})^{\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}|\theta|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}.\end{split}

(197)

Furthermore, we get from the
Burkholder-Davis-Gundy inequality (see, e.g., Theorem A in
[
9
]
)
for all
t\in[0,T]
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Big\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{(t-u)A}\big((D\Pi)(X_{\kappa(u),u})(B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)}))\big)\,\mathrm{d}W_{u}\Big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\gamma})}\\
\leq{}&\Big\|2\sqrt{p}\,\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\big\|e^{(t-u)A}(-A)^{\gamma}\big((D\Pi)(X_{\kappa(u),u})(B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)}))\big)\big\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}\,\mathrm{d}u\Big)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\\
={}&\Big\|2\sqrt{p}\,\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}e^{2(t-u)\lambda_{i}}(-\lambda_{i})^{2\gamma}\big\|B^{*}(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})((D\Pi)(X_{\kappa(u),u}))^{*}e_{i}\big\|^{2}_{U}\,\mathrm{d}u\Big)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}.\end{split}

(198)

Next note that
(
191
),
(
45
),
the fact that

\forall\lambda\in(0,\infty)\colon\lambda^{\gamma}e^{-\lambda}\leq(\tfrac{\gamma}{e})^{\gamma}
,
and Lemma
3.3

(with
\gamma\curvearrowleft 0

and with
\delta\curvearrowleft 0
)
imply for all
t\in[0,T]
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Big\|\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{I}}e^{2(t-u)\lambda_{i}}(-\lambda_{i})^{2\gamma}\big\|B^{*}(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})((D\Pi)(X_{\kappa(u),u}))^{*}e_{i}\big\|^{2}_{U}\,\mathrm{d}u\Big\|_{L^{p/2}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\\
\leq{}&\Big\|\int_{0}^{t}\big(\sup_{\lambda\in(0,\infty)}e^{-(t-u)\lambda}\lambda^{\gamma}\big)^{2}\big\|B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})((D\Pi)(X_{\kappa(u),u}))\big\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}\,\mathrm{d}u\Big\|_{L^{p/2}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\\
\leq{}&\Big\|\int_{0}^{t}(t-u)^{-2\gamma}(\tfrac{\gamma}{e})^{2\gamma}\big\|(D\Pi)(X_{\kappa(u),u})\big\|^{2}_{L(H,H)}\big\|B(\kappa(u),Y_{\kappa(u)})\big\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}\,\mathrm{d}u\Big\|_{L^{p/2}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\\
\leq{}&\int_{0}^{t}(t-u)^{-2\gamma}(c_{1}\|X_{\kappa(u),u}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H)}+1)^{2}\eta^{2}(\tfrac{\gamma}{e})^{2\gamma}\,\mathrm{d}u\\
\leq{}&\int_{0}^{t}(t-u)^{-2\gamma}(c_{1}\eta\,p\,|\theta|^{1/2}+1)^{2}\eta^{2}(\tfrac{\gamma}{e})^{2\gamma}\,\mathrm{d}u={}t^{1-2\gamma}\tfrac{(c_{1}\eta\,p\,|\theta|^{1/2}+1)^{2}\eta^{2}}{1-2\gamma}(\tfrac{\gamma}{e})^{2\gamma}.\end{split}

(199)

Combining
(
198
),
(
199
),
Lemma
3.5
(with
c_{1}\curvearrowleft c_{3}
),
and the fact that

\forall x\in H\colon\|x\|_{H}\leq(\inf_{i\in\mathcal{I}}|\lambda_{i}|)^{\gamma-\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}

demonstrates for all

t\in[0,T]
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Big\|\int_{0}^{t}e^{(t-u)A}\big((D\Pi)(X_{\llcorner u\lrcorner_{\theta},u})(B(\llcorner u\lrcorner_{\theta},Y_{\llcorner u\lrcorner_{\theta}}))\big)\,\mathrm{d}W_{u}\Big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\gamma})}\\
\leq{}&2\sqrt{p}\min\Big\{t^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}-\gamma}\tfrac{(c_{1}+1)\eta}{\sqrt{1-2\gamma}}(\tfrac{\gamma}{e})^{\gamma},(\inf_{i\in\mathcal{I}}|\lambda_{i}|)^{\gamma-\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Big(c_{3}\eta+l(\kappa)\\
&\qquad+(\tfrac{1-2\gamma_{2}}{e})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}-\gamma_{2}}c\eta^{2}\,p(\tfrac{\gamma_{2}}{e})^{\gamma_{2}}t^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{1-2\gamma_{2}}}\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{2\gamma_{2}}}\Big)\Big\}.\end{split}

(200)

Thus,
(
46
),
(
196
), (
194
),
(
197
), and
(
200
)
prove for all
t\in[0,T]
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|Y_{t}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\gamma})}\leq{}&\|Y_{0}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\gamma})}+\tfrac{c_{2}}{1-(\gamma-\alpha_{1})}(\tfrac{\gamma-\alpha_{1}}{e})^{\gamma-\alpha_{1}}t^{1-(\gamma-\alpha_{1})}(1+\sup_{u\in\theta}\|Y_{\llcorner u\lrcorner_{\theta}}\|^{q_{2}}_{L^{pq_{2}}(\mathbbm{P};H_{\alpha_{2}})})\\
&+c_{1}+\tfrac{c_{1}\eta^{3}p}{1-\gamma}\,(\tfrac{\gamma}{e})^{\gamma}t^{1-\gamma}|\theta|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\\
&+2\sqrt{p}\min\Big\{t^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}-\gamma}\tfrac{(c_{1}+1)\eta}{\sqrt{1-2\gamma}}(\tfrac{\gamma}{e})^{\gamma},c_{3}(\inf_{i\in\mathcal{I}}|\lambda_{i}|)^{\gamma-\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\eta+l(\kappa)\\
&\qquad+(\tfrac{\gamma-\gamma_{2}}{e})^{\gamma-\gamma_{2}}c_{1}\eta^{2}\,p(\tfrac{\gamma_{2}}{e})^{\gamma_{2}}t^{1-\gamma}\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{1-2\gamma_{2}}}\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{1+2\gamma_{2}-2\gamma}}\Big\}.\end{split}

(201)

This finishes the proof of Lemma
3.12
.
∎

The next lemma establishes one step
exponential moment bounds for
integrated tamed exponential Euler approximations
with respect to the
\|\cdot\|_{H_{1/2}}
-norm.

Lemma 3.13
.

Assume Setting
3.1
,
let
c_{1},c_{2},{\varepsilon}\in(0,\infty)
,
\alpha\in[0,\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}})
,

\gamma\in(-\infty,0]
satisfy that

48\,{\varepsilon}\,e\,c_{1}|\theta|\eta^{2}\leq\tfrac{1}{2},

(202)

let
\kappa=([0,T]\ni t\to\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}\in[0,T])
,
assume for all
t\in[0,T]
and all
x,y\in D
that

\displaystyle\|x\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}

\displaystyle\leq c_{2}|\theta|^{\gamma},

(203)

\displaystyle\|F(t,x)\|_{H_{-\alpha}}

\displaystyle\leq c_{2}|\theta|^{\gamma},

(204)

and assume for all
x\in H
that

\displaystyle\|\Pi(x)\|_{H_{1/2}}\leq c_{1}\|x\|_{H_{1/2}}.

(205)

Then it holds
for all
t\in[0,T]
and all
s\in(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},t]

that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Big\|\mathbbm{1}_{D}(Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\exp\Big(\int_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}^{s}{\varepsilon}\|Y_{r}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}\,\mathrm{d}r\Big)\Big\|^{4}_{L^{4}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\\
\leq{}&\exp\Big((s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})^{1+\gamma}12{\varepsilon}\,\big(c_{2}+(\tfrac{1+2\alpha}{2e})^{2\alpha+1}\tfrac{4c_{2}^{2}}{(1-2\alpha)^{2}}|\theta|^{1-2\alpha+\gamma}+8e\,c_{1}|\theta|^{-\gamma}\eta^{2}\big)\Big).\end{split}

(206)

Proof.

First note that Lemma
3.2
,
and (
205
) imply for all

t\in[0,T]
and all
s\in(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},t]
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Big\|\mathbbm{1}_{D}(Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\exp\Big(\int_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}^{s}{\varepsilon}\|Y_{r}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}\,\mathrm{d}r\Big)\Big\|^{4}_{L^{4}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\\
={}&\mathbbm{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}_{D}(Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\exp\Big(4\int_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}^{s}{\varepsilon}\Big\|e^{(r-\llcorner r\lrcorner_{\theta})A}Y_{\llcorner r\lrcorner_{\theta}}+\int^{r}_{\llcorner r\lrcorner_{\theta}}e^{(r-u)A}F(\llcorner r\lrcorner_{\theta},Y_{\llcorner r\lrcorner_{\theta}})\,\mathrm{d}u\\
&\quad+\Pi\Big(\int^{r}_{\llcorner r\lrcorner_{\theta}}e^{(r-u)A}B(\llcorner r\lrcorner_{\theta},Y_{\llcorner r\lrcorner_{\theta}})\,\mathrm{d}W_{u}\Big)\Big\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}\,\mathrm{d}r\Big)\bigg]\\
\leq{}&\mathbbm{E}\bigg[\mathbbm{1}_{D}(Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\exp\Big(12{\varepsilon}\int_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}^{s}\Big\|e^{(r-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})A}Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}\Big\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}+12{\varepsilon}\Big\|\int^{r}_{\llcorner r\lrcorner_{\theta}}e^{(r-u)A}F(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\,\mathrm{d}u\Big\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}\\
&\quad+12{\varepsilon}c_{1}\Big\|\int^{r}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}e^{(r-u)A}B(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\,\mathrm{d}W_{u}\Big\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}\,\mathrm{d}r\Big)\bigg].\end{split}

(207)

Next note that
Lemma
2.3

(with

x\curvearrowleft F(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})
,

\gamma\curvearrowleft\tfrac{1}{2}+\alpha
and with
\delta\curvearrowleft\tfrac{1}{2}
)
ensures for all
t\in[0,T]

and all
s\in[t,T]
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\int_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}^{s}\Big\|e^{(r-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})A}Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}\Big\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}+\Big\|\int^{r}_{\llcorner r\lrcorner_{\theta}}e^{(r-u)A}F(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\,\mathrm{d}u\Big\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}\,\mathrm{d}r\\
\leq{}&(s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})\|Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}+(\tfrac{1+2\alpha}{2e})^{2\alpha+1}\tfrac{4}{(1-2\alpha)^{2}}\int_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}^{s}(r-\llcorner r\lrcorner_{\theta})^{1-2\alpha}\|F(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\|^{2}_{H_{-\alpha}}\,\mathrm{d}r\\
\leq{}&(s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})\|Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}+(\tfrac{1+2\alpha}{2e})^{2\alpha+1}\tfrac{4}{(1-2\alpha)^{2}}(s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})|\theta|^{1-2\alpha}\|F(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\|^{2}_{H_{-\alpha}}.\end{split}

(208)

Moreover, Lemma
2.4

(with

Q\curvearrowleft\sqrt{12{\varepsilon}}\,B(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})
),
(
45
),
(
202
),
the fact that for all
{x\in[0,\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}]}

it holds that
\tfrac{1}{1-x}\leq 1+2x
verify for all

t\in[0,T]
and all
s\in(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},t]
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbbm{E}\Big[\exp\big(12{\varepsilon}c_{1}\int_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}^{s}\Big\|\int^{r}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}e^{(r-u)A}B(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\,\mathrm{d}W_{u}\Big\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}\,\mathrm{d}r\Big)~\Big|~Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}\Big]\\
\leq{}&\tfrac{1}{s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}\int_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}^{s}\mathbbm{E}\bigg[\frac{1}{\big(1-48{\varepsilon}\,e\,c_{1}(s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})\|B(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}\big)^{+}}~\Big|~~Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}\bigg]\,\mathrm{d}r\\
\leq{}&\tfrac{1}{s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}\int_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}^{s}1+96{\varepsilon}\,e\,c_{1}(s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})\eta^{2}\,\mathrm{d}r\\
={}&1+96{\varepsilon}\,e\,c_{1}(s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})\eta^{2}.\end{split}

(209)

Thus, (
207
),
(
208
),
(
209
),
the fact that for all
x\in\mathbbm{R}
it holds that
1+x\leq e^{x}
,
(
203
), and (
204
)
prove for all
t\in[0,T]
and all
s\in(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},t]
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Big\|\mathbbm{1}_{D}(Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\exp\Big(\int_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}^{s}{\varepsilon}\|Y_{r}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}\,\mathrm{d}r\Big)\Big\|^{4}_{L^{4}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\\
\leq{}&\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbbm{1}_{D}(Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\exp\Big(12{\varepsilon}(s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})\|Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}+(\tfrac{1+2\alpha}{2e})^{2\alpha+1}\tfrac{48{\varepsilon}}{(1-2\alpha)^{2}}(s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})|\theta|^{1-2\alpha}\\
&\quad\cdot\|F(\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta},Y_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta}})\|^{2}_{H_{-\alpha}}\Big)(1+96{\varepsilon}\,e\,c_{1}(s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})\eta^{2})\Big]\\
\leq{}&\exp\Big(12{\varepsilon}(s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})c_{2}|\theta|^{\gamma}+(\tfrac{1+2\alpha}{2e})^{2\alpha+1}\tfrac{48{\varepsilon}}{(1-2\alpha)^{2}}(s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})|\theta|^{1-2\alpha}c_{2}^{2}|\theta|^{2\gamma}\Big)\\
&\quad\cdot\exp(96{\varepsilon}\,e\,c_{1}(s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})\eta^{2})\\
\leq{}&\exp\Big((s-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{\theta})^{1+\gamma}12{\varepsilon}\,\big(c_{2}+(\tfrac{1+2\alpha}{2e})^{2\alpha+1}\tfrac{4c_{2}^{2}}{(1-2\alpha)^{2}}|\theta|^{1-2\alpha+\gamma}+8e\,c_{1}|\theta|^{-\gamma}\eta^{2}\big)\Big).\end{split}

(210)

This completes the proof of Lemma
3.13
.
∎

4
A generalized perturbation estimate for SDEs

The next lemma is a generalization of Lemma 2.10 in Hutzenthaler
\&
Jentzen

[
24
]
. With
\psi=(H^{2}\ni(x,y)\mapsto\mu(y)\in H)

and
\varphi=(H^{2}\ni(x,y)\mapsto\sigma(y)\in HS(U,H))
we obtain
Lemma 2.10 in Hutzenthaler
\&
Jentzen

[
24
]
.

Lemma 4.1
.

Let
T\in(0,\infty)
,
{\varepsilon}\in[0,\infty]
,
p\in[2,\infty)
,
let

(H,\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{H},\|\cdot\|_{H})
and

(U,\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{U},\|\cdot\|_{U})

be separable
\mathbbm{R}
-Hilbert spaces with
\#H\wedge\#U>1
,
let

(\Omega,\mathcal{F},\mathbbm{P})

be a probability space with a normal filtration

\mathbb{F}=(\mathbb{F}_{t})_{t\in[0,T]}
,
let

(W_{t})_{t\in[0,T]}

be an
\operatorname{Id}_{U}
-cylindrical

\mathbb{F}
-Wiener
process with continuous sample paths,
let
\sigma\colon H\to HS(U,H)
,
\mu\colon H\to H

\psi\colon H^{2}\to H
,
\varphi\colon H^{2}\to HS(U,H)

be measurable,
let
\tau\colon\Omega\to[0,T]

be a stopping time, let
X,Y\colon[0,T]\times\Omega\to H
be adapted stochastic processes with
continuous sample paths, let
a\colon[0,T]\times\Omega\to H
,

b\colon[0,T]\times\Omega\to HS(U,H)
,

\chi\colon[0,T]\times\Omega\to\mathbbm{R}
be predictale stochastic processes and assume that for all

t\in[0,T]
it holds a.s. that

\int_{0}^{T}\|a_{s}\|_{H}+\|b_{s}\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}+\|\mu(X_{s})\|_{H}+\|\sigma(X_{s})\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}+\|\psi(X_{s},Y_{s})\|_{H}+\|\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}\,\mathrm{d}s<\infty
,

X_{t}=X_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}\mu(X_{s})\,\mathrm{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}\sigma(X_{s})\,\mathrm{d}W_{s}<\infty
,

Y_{t}=Y_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}a_{s}\,\mathrm{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}b_{s}\,\mathrm{d}W_{s}<\infty
,
and

\int_{0}^{\tau}\bigg[\frac{\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\mu(X_{s})-\psi(X_{s},Y_{s})\rangle_{H}+\frac{(p-1)(1+{\varepsilon})}{2}\|\sigma(X_{s})-\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}}{\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|^{2}_{H}}+\chi_{s}\bigg]^{+}\,\mathrm{d}s<\infty.

(211)

Then for all
r,q\in(0,\infty]
with
\tfrac{1}{p}+\tfrac{1}{q}=\tfrac{1}{r}

it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|X_{\tau}-Y_{\tau}\|_{L^{r}(\Omega;H)}\\
\leq{}&\bigg\|\exp\Big(\int_{0}^{\tau}\Big[\frac{\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\mu(X_{s})-\psi(X_{s},Y_{s})\rangle_{H}+\tfrac{(p-1)(1+{\varepsilon})}{2}\|\sigma(X_{s})-\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}}{\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|^{2}_{H}}\\
&\qquad\qquad+\chi_{s}\Big]^{+}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\bigg\|_{L^{q}(\Omega;\mathbbm{R})}\cdot\bigg[\|X_{0}-Y_{0}\|_{L^{p}(\Omega;H)}+\Big(\mathbbm{E}\Big[\int_{0}^{\tau}p\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}\\
&\qquad\Big[\big\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\psi(X_{s},Y_{s})-a_{s}\big\rangle_{H}+\tfrac{(p-1)(1+1/{\varepsilon})}{2}\|\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})-b_{s}\|_{HS(U,H)}^{2}\\
&\qquad\qquad-\chi_{s}\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{2}\Big]^{+}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big]\Big)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{p}}}\bigg].\end{split}

(212)

Proof.

First denote by
\hat{\chi}\colon[0,T]\times\Omega\to[0,\infty)

the process satisfying for all
t\in[0,T]
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\hat{\chi}_{t}={}&p\mathbbm{1}_{(t,T]}(\tau)\bigg[\frac{2\langle X_{t}-Y_{t},\mu(X_{t})-\psi(X_{t},Y_{t})\rangle_{H}}{2\|X_{t}-Y_{t}\|^{2}_{H}}\\
&+\frac{(p-1)(1+{\varepsilon})\|\sigma(X_{t})-\varphi(X_{t},Y_{t})\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}}{2\|X_{t}-Y_{t}\|^{2}_{H}}+\chi_{t}\bigg]^{+}.\end{split}

(213)

We note that
\hat{\chi}
is predictable.
Then Proposition 2.5 in Hutzenthaler
\&
Jentzen

[
24
]

(with
V\curvearrowleft(H^{2}\ni(x,y)\mapsto\|x-y\|_{H}^{p}\in\mathbbm{R})
and with

\chi\curvearrowleft\hat{\chi}
),
Remark 2.14 in Cox, Hutzenthaler
\&
Jentzen
[
11
]

and a straightforward generalization of Example 2.15
in Cox, Hutzenthaler
\&
Jentzen
[
11
]

imply
that a.s. it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{\|X_{\tau}-Y_{\tau}\|_{H}^{p}}{\exp(\int_{0}^{\tau}\hat{\chi}_{s}\,\mathrm{d}s)}=\|X_{0}-Y_{0}\|_{H}^{p}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\tfrac{p\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}}{\exp(\int_{0}^{s}\hat{\chi}_{u}\,\mathrm{d}u)}\big\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},(\sigma(X_{s})-b_{s})\,\mathrm{d}W_{s}\big\rangle\\
&+\int_{0}^{\tau}\tfrac{p(p-2)\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-4}}{2\exp(\int_{0}^{s}\hat{\chi}_{u}\,\mathrm{d}u)}\|(\sigma(X_{s})-b_{s})^{*}(X_{s}-Y_{s})\|_{U}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\\
&+\int_{0}^{\tau}\tfrac{p\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}}{\exp(\int_{0}^{s}\hat{\chi}_{u}\,\mathrm{d}u)}\Big(\big\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\mu(X_{s})-a_{s}\big\rangle_{H}+\tfrac{1}{2}\|\sigma(X_{s})-b_{s}\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}\\
&\qquad\qquad\qquad-\hat{\chi}_{s}\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|^{2}_{H}\Big)\,\mathrm{d}s.\end{split}

(214)

Moreover, note that Young’s inequality ensures for all

s\in[0,T]
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\tfrac{p(p-2)}{2}\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-4}\|(\sigma(X_{s})-b_{s})^{*}(X_{s}-Y_{s})\|_{U}^{2}\\
&+p\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}\Big(\big\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\mu(X_{s})-a_{s}\big\rangle_{H}+\tfrac{1}{2}\|\sigma(X_{s})-b_{s}\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}\Big)\\
={}&\tfrac{p(p-2)}{2}\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-4}\big(\|(\sigma(X_{s})-\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s}))^{*}(X_{s}-Y_{s})\|_{U}^{2}+\|(\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})-b_{s})^{*}(X_{s}-Y_{s})\|_{U}^{2}\big)\\
&+p(p-2)\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-4}\big\langle\big((\sigma(X_{s})-\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s}))^{*}(X_{s}-Y_{s}),(\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})-b_{s})^{*}(X_{s}-Y_{s})\big\rangle_{U}\\
&+\tfrac{p}{2}\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}\big(\|\sigma(X_{s})-\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}+\|\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})-b_{s}\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}\big)\\
&+p\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}\langle\sigma(X_{s})-\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s}),\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})-b_{s}\rangle_{HS(U,H)}\\
&+p\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}\big(\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\mu(X_{s})-\psi(X_{s},Y_{s})\rangle_{H}+\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\psi(X_{s},Y_{s})-a_{s}\rangle_{H}\big)\\
\leq{}&\tfrac{p(p-1)}{2}\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}\big(\|\sigma(X_{s})-\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})\|_{HS(U,H)}^{2}+\|\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})-b_{s}\|_{HS(U,H)}^{2}\\
&\qquad\qquad+2\|\sigma(X_{s})-\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})\|_{HS(U,H)}\|\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})-b_{s}\|_{HS(U,H)}\big)\\
&+p\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}\big(\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\mu(X_{s})-\psi(X_{s},Y_{s})\rangle_{H}+\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\psi(X_{s},Y_{s})-a_{s}\rangle_{H}\big)\\
\leq{}&\tfrac{p(p-1)}{2}\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}\big((1+{\varepsilon})\|\sigma(X_{s})-\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})\|_{HS(U,H)}^{2}+(1+\tfrac{1}{{\varepsilon}})\|\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})-b_{s}\|_{HS(U,H)}^{2}\big)\\
&+p\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}\big(\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\mu(X_{s})-\psi(X_{s},Y_{s})\rangle_{H}+\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\psi(X_{s},Y_{s})-a_{s}\rangle_{H}\big).\end{split}

(215)

Combining (
214
),
(
215
), and
(
213
) proves that
a.s. it holds that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{\|X_{\tau}-Y_{\tau}\|_{H}^{p}}{\exp(\int_{0}^{\tau}\hat{\chi}_{s}\,\mathrm{d}s)}\leq\|X_{0}-Y_{0}\|_{H}^{p}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\tfrac{p\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}}{\exp(\int_{0}^{s}\hat{\chi}_{u}\,\mathrm{d}u)}\big\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},(\sigma(X_{s})-b_{s})\,\mathrm{d}W_{s}\big\rangle\\
&+\int_{0}^{\tau}\tfrac{p\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}}{2\exp(\int_{0}^{s}\hat{\chi}_{u}\,\mathrm{d}u)}\Big[2\big\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\psi(X_{s},Y_{s})-a_{s}\big\rangle_{H}+(p-1)(1+\tfrac{1}{{\varepsilon}})\|\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})-b_{s}\|_{HS(U,H)}^{2}\\
&\qquad-\chi_{s}\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{2}\Big]^{+}\,\mathrm{d}s.\end{split}

(216)

Thus a localization of the stochastic integral and Fatou’s lemma verify that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathbbm{E}\bigg[\frac{\|X_{\tau}-Y_{\tau}\|_{H}^{p}}{\exp(\int_{0}^{\tau}\hat{\chi}_{s}\,\mathrm{d}s)}\bigg]\\
\leq{}&\mathbbm{E}[\|X_{0}-Y_{0}\|_{H}^{p}]+\mathbbm{E}\bigg[\int_{0}^{\tau}\tfrac{p\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}}{2\exp(\int_{0}^{s}\hat{\chi}_{u}\,\mathrm{d}u)}\Big[2\big\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\psi(X_{s},Y_{s})-a_{s}\big\rangle_{H}\\
&\qquad+(p-1)(1+\tfrac{1}{{\varepsilon}})\|\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})-b_{s}\|_{HS(U,H)}^{2}-\chi_{s}\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{2}\Big]^{+}\,\mathrm{d}s\bigg].\end{split}

(217)

Therefore Hölder’s inequality and the fact that
\hat{\chi}\geq 0

show for all
q,r\in(0,\infty]
with

\tfrac{1}{p}+\tfrac{1}{q}=\tfrac{1}{r}
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|X_{\tau}-Y_{\tau}\|^{p}_{L^{r}(\mathbbm{P};H)}\leq\Big\|\exp\Big(\tfrac{1}{p}\int_{0}^{\tau}\hat{\chi}_{s}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\Big\|^{p}_{L^{q}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\bigg\|\frac{\|X_{\tau}-Y_{\tau}\|_{H}}{\exp(\frac{1}{p}\int_{0}^{\tau}\hat{\chi}_{s}\,\mathrm{d}s)}\bigg\|^{p}_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\\
\leq{}&\Big\|\exp\Big(\tfrac{1}{p}\int_{0}^{\tau}\hat{\chi}_{s}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\Big\|^{p}_{L^{q}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\mathbbm{E}\bigg[\|X_{0}-Y_{0}\|_{H}^{p}+\int_{0}^{\tau}p\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{p-2}\\
&\qquad\Big[\big\langle X_{s}-Y_{s},\psi(X_{s},Y_{s})-a_{s}\big\rangle_{H}+\tfrac{(p-1)(1+1/{\varepsilon})}{2}\|\varphi(X_{s},Y_{s})-b_{s}\|_{HS(U,H)}^{2}\\
&\qquad-\chi_{s}\|X_{s}-Y_{s}\|_{H}^{2}\Big]^{+}\,\mathrm{d}s\bigg].\end{split}

(218)

This completes the prove of Lemma
4.1
.
∎

5
Strong convergence rates for stochastic Burgers equations

In this section we apply our results to stochastic Burgers equations with
zero Dirichlet boundary conditions and
multiplicative noise.
More precisely we apply Proposition
3.8

to prove in Proposition
5.4

exponential moment bounds for
tamed exponential Euler approximations
of stochastic Burgers equations.
This together with Lemma
4.1

is used in Proposition
5.5
to derive
strong temporal convergence rate
1/2
of tamed exponential Euler approximations
of stochastic Burgers equations.
In Lemma
5.6
we prove
sufficient conditions for the taming functions
\Pi^{n}
,
n\in\mathbbm{N}
.
Finally, we combine these results with Corollary 3.11 in
Hutzenthaler & Jentzen
[
24
]

to establish in Corollary
5.7
strong temporal convergence rate
1/2

and strong spatial convergence rate
1-
.

Throughout this section the following setting is frequently used.

Setting 5.1
.

Let
T,\eta,\varsigma,c_{2}\in(0,\infty)
,
c_{1}\in\mathbbm{R}
,

\gamma_{1}\in[-\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}},0)
,
{\varepsilon}\in(0,(96Tec_{2}\eta^{2})^{-1}\wedge 1]
,
for every
n\in\mathbbm{N}
denote by

\llcorner\cdot\lrcorner_{n}\colon[0,T]\to[0,T]
the function
satisfying for all
t\in(0,T]
that

\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}=\max\{\tfrac{Tk}{n}\colon k\in\mathbbm{N}_{0},~\tfrac{Tk}{n}<t\}

and that
\llcorner 0\lrcorner_{n}=0
,
let
H=L^{2}((0,1);\mathbbm{R})
,
let
(e_{i})_{i\in\mathbbm{N}}\subseteq H

satisfy for all
i\in\mathbbm{N}
and
Lebesgue-a.a.
x\in(0,1)
that

e_{i}(x)=\sqrt{2}\sin(i\pi x),

let
(\lambda_{i})_{i\in\mathbbm{N}}\subseteq(-\infty,0)

satisfy that

(\lambda_{i})_{i\in\mathbbm{N}}=(-\pi^{2}i^{2})_{i\in\mathbbm{N}}
,
let
P_{N}\colon H\to H
,
N\in\mathbbm{N}_{0}
,
satisfy for all
x\in H
and all
N\in\mathbbm{N}
that

P_{N}(x)=\sum^{N}_{i=1}\langle e_{i},x\rangle_{H}e_{i},

and that

P_{0}(x)=\operatorname{Id}_{H},

let

A\colon D(A)\subseteq H\rightarrow H

be a linear operator satisfying that

D(A)=\biggl\{x\in H\colon\sum^{\infty}_{i=1}\left|\lambda_{i}\langle e_{i},x\rangle_{H}\right|^{2}<\infty\biggr\}

(219)

and that for all
v\in D(A)
it holds that

Av=\sum^{\infty}_{i=1}\lambda_{i}\langle e_{i},v\rangle_{H}\,e_{i},

(220)

let
A_{N}\colon H\to H
,
N\in\mathbbm{N}_{0}
, be
linear operators
satisfying for all

x\in H
and all
N\in\mathbbm{N}_{0}
that

A_{N}(x)=\sum_{i\in\mathbbm{N}}\lambda_{i\wedge N}\langle x,e_{i}\rangle_{H}e_{i}
,
let

(H_{r},\left<\cdot,\cdot\right>_{H_{r}},\left\|\cdot\right\|_{H_{r}})
,

r\in\mathbbm{R}
,
be a family of interpolation spaces associated with

-A

(see, e.g., Definition 3.6.30 in Jentzen
[
33
]
),
for every
r\in\mathbbm{R}

extend the norm
\|\cdot\|_{H_{r}}

to a semi-norm

\|\cdot\|_{H_{r}}\colon\bigcup_{i=1}^{\infty}H_{-i}\to[0,\infty]
,
let

F\in\operatorname{C}(H,H_{-1})
be a function satisfying for all

x\in H_{1}
that

F(x)=-c_{1}x^{\prime}x
, let
F_{N}\colon H\to H
,
N\in\mathbbm{N}_{0}
,
satisfy for all

x\in H
and all
N\in\mathbbm{N}_{0}
that

F_{N}(x)=P_{N}F(P_{N}x)
,
let

B\colon H\to HS(H,H)

satisfy that

\sup_{x\in H}\|B(x)\|_{HS(H,H)}\leq\eta

(221)

and that for all
x,y\in H
it holds that

\|B(x)-B(y)\|_{HS(H,H)}\leq\varsigma\|x-y\|_{H},

(222)

let
B_{N}\colon H\to HS(H,H)
,
N\in\mathbbm{N}_{0}
,
satisfy for all

x\in H
and all
N\in\mathbbm{N}_{0}
that

B_{N}(x)=P_{N}B(P_{N}x)P_{N}
,
let
V\in\operatorname{C}^{1,2}([0,T]\times H,[0,\infty))

satisfy for all
(t,x)\in[0,T]\times H
that

V(t,x)=e^{-2\eta^{2}t}(\|x\|^{2}_{H}+1)
,
let
\overline{V}\colon H_{1/2}\to\mathbbm{R}

satisfy for all
x\in H_{1}
that

\overline{V}(x)=e^{-2\eta^{2}T}{\varepsilon}\|x\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}
,
let

(\Omega,\mathcal{F},\mathbbm{P})

be a probability space with a normal filtration

\mathbb{F}=(\mathbb{F}_{t})_{t\in[0,T]}
,
let

(W_{t})_{t\in[0,T]}

be an
\operatorname{Id}_{H}
-cylindrical

\mathbb{F}
-Wiener
process with continuous sample paths,
let
D\colon\mathbbm{N}\to\mathcal{B}(H)
satisfy for all
n\in\mathbbm{N}
that

D_{n}=\{x\in H\colon\|x\|_{H_{1/2}}\vee\|x\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}\leq\big(\tfrac{T}{n}\big)^{\gamma_{1}}\},

(223)

let
\Pi^{n}\in\operatorname{C}^{2}(H,H)
,
n\in\mathbbm{N}
,
let
L^{n}\colon H\to L(H,H)
,
n\in\mathbbm{N}
, be measurable
and satisfy that

\sup_{n\in\mathbbm{N}}\|L^{n}(x)\|_{L(H,H)}<\infty

(224)

and that
for all

and all

it holds that

L^{n}(x)e_{i}=\langle L^{n}(x)e_{i},e_{i}\rangle_{H}e_{i},

(225)

and let
Y^{N,n}\colon[0,T]\times\Omega\to H
,

,
be adapted stochastic processes with continuous sample paths
satisfying for all
N\in\mathbbm{N}
and all

that

\sup_{N,n\in\mathbbm{N}}\|Y_{0}^{N,n}\|_{L^{\infty}(\mathbbm{P};H_{1/2})}<\infty
and
for all
N\in\mathbbm{N}
,

,
and all

that a.s. it holds that

\begin{split}Y_{t}^{N,n}={}&e^{(t-\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n})A_{N}}Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}}+\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\int^{t}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}}e^{(t-s)A_{N}}F_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\,\mathrm{d}s\\
&+\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\Pi^{n}\Big(\int^{t}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}}e^{(t-s)A_{N}}B_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\,\mathrm{d}W_{s}\Big).\end{split}

(226)

In the next lemma we apply Lemma
3.12
to get
moment estimate with respect to the
\|\cdot\|_{H_{1/2}}
-norm.
of the galerkin approximation.

Lemma 5.2
.

Assume Setting
5.1
,
let
p\in[2,\infty)
,
let
X^{N}\colon[0,T]\times\Omega\to H
,
N\in\mathbbm{N}
,
be adapted stochastic processes with continuous sample paths
satisfying for all
N\in\mathbbm{N}

and all
t\in[0,T]
a.s. that

X_{t}^{N}={}e^{tA_{N}}P_{N}(X^{N}_{0})+\int^{t}_{0}e^{(t-s)A_{N}}F_{N}(X^{N}_{s})\,\mathrm{d}s+\int^{t}_{0}e^{(t-s)A_{N}}B_{N}(X^{N}_{s})\,\mathrm{d}W_{s}.

(227)

Then it holds that

\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\|X_{t}^{N}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P},H_{1/2})}<\infty.

(228)

Proof.

First note, that Corollary 2.4 in in Cox, Hutzenthaler & Jentzen
[
11
]

and Lemma 3.1-3.2 in Jentzen & Pušnik
[
37
]
prove that

\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\|X_{t}^{N}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P},H_{4/15})}<\infty.

(229)

Furthermore, Corollary 4.22 (with
\alpha_{1}\curvearrowleft\tfrac{91}{120}
)
in Jentzen, Lindner & Pus̆nik

[
36
]

ensure
that there exists a
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
x\in H
and all
N\in\mathbbm{N}

it holds that

\displaystyle\|F_{N}(x)\|_{H_{-91/120}}\leq\|F(P_{N}(x))\|_{H_{-91/120}}\leq C\|P_{N}(x)\|^{2}_{H}\leq C\|x\|^{2}_{H}.

(230)

Moreover,
Lemma 4.16 in Jentzen, Lindner & Pus̆nik

[
36
]

(with
\gamma\curvearrowleft\tfrac{4}{15}
,
\vartheta\curvearrowleft\tfrac{1}{3}
,

v\curvearrowleft x
,
w\curvearrowleft 0
)
verifies that there exists a
C\in(0,\infty)

such that for all
x\in H
and all
N\in\mathbbm{N}

it holds that

\displaystyle\|F_{N}(x)\|_{H_{-1/3}}\leq C\|P_{N}(x)\|_{H_{4/15}}(1+\|P_{N}(x)\|_{H_{4/15}})\leq 2C(1+\|x\|^{2}_{H_{4/15}}).

(231)

Therefore, Lemma
3.11

(with
\Pi\curvearrowleft\operatorname{Id}_{H}
,
\kappa\curvearrowleft\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbbm{R}}
,

\alpha\curvearrowleft 91/120
,
\alpha_{1}\curvearrowleft 0
,

q\curvearrowleft 2
),
(
230
),
(
229
),
and the fact that
\forall x\in H\colon\|x\|_{29/120}\leq\|x\|_{H_{4/15}}

demonstrate
for all
q\in[2,\infty)
that

\sup_{s,t\in[0,T]}\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\tfrac{\|X^{N}_{t}-X^{N}_{s}\|_{L^{q}(\mathbbm{P};H)}}{|t-s|^{29/120}}<\infty.

(232)

In addition,
Lemma
3.12

(with
\Pi\curvearrowleft\operatorname{Id}_{H}
,
\kappa\curvearrowleft\operatorname{Id}_{\mathbbm{R}}
,

\alpha\curvearrowleft 91/120
,
\alpha_{1}\curvearrowleft-1/3
,

\alpha_{2}\curvearrowleft 4/15
,

\gamma\curvearrowleft 1/2
,

q_{1}\curvearrowleft 2
,
q_{2}\curvearrowleft 2
),
Lemma
3.6
,
(
232
),
(
222
),
(
230
),
(
231
),
(
229
)
imply that

\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\|X^{N}_{t}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{1/2})}<\infty.

(233)

This completes the proof of Lemma
5.2
.
∎

In the next lemma we establish spatial convergence rate
1
.
It is a direct consequence of Lemma
5.2

and well known results.

Corollary 5.3
.

Assume Setting
5.1
,
let
p\in[2,\infty)
,
let
X^{N}\colon[0,T]\times\Omega\to H
,
N\in\mathbbm{N}_{0}
,
be adapted stochastic processes with continuous sample paths
satisfying for all
N\in\mathbbm{N}_{0}

and all
t\in[0,T]
a.s. that

X_{t}^{N}={}e^{tA_{N}}P_{N}(X^{N}_{0})+\int^{t}_{0}e^{(t-s)A_{N}}F_{N}(X^{N}_{s})\,\mathrm{d}s+\int^{t}_{0}e^{(t-s)A_{N}}B_{N}(X^{N}_{s})\,\mathrm{d}W_{s}.

(234)

Then there exists a
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
N\in\mathbbm{N}_{0}
it holds that

\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\|X_{t}^{0}-X_{t}^{N}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P},H)}\leq C\,N^{-1}

(235)

Proof.

This follows directly from Lemma
5.2
and
Display (107) in Corollary 3.11 in Hutzenthaler & Jentzen
[
24
]

(with
\alpha\curvearrowleft 1
,
r\curvearrowleft p
). ∎

In the next proposition we apply Proposition
3.8

to get exponential moment bounds for tamed exponential Euler approximations
of stochastic Burgers equations.

Proposition 5.4
.

Assume Setting
5.1
,
let
c_{3}\in[1,\infty)
,

p\in[4,\infty)
,

\gamma_{2}\in[\tfrac{-1-2\gamma_{1}}{2},\gamma_{1}+\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}]
,

\gamma_{3}\in[0,\infty)
,

\gamma_{4}\in(0,\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}})
,
for every
s\in[0,T]
let

X^{N,n}_{s}\colon[s,T]\times\Omega\to H
,
N,n\in\mathbbm{N}
, be an
adapted stochastic process with continuous sample paths
satisfying that for all
t\in[s,T]
it holds a.s. that

X^{N,n}_{s,t}=\int_{s}^{t}e^{(t-u)A_{N}}B_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner u\lrcorner_{n}})\,\mathrm{d}W_{u},

(236)

assume for all
x\in H
,
z\in HS(H,H)
and all
n\in\mathbbm{N}
that

\displaystyle\|\Pi^{n}(x)\|_{H}\leq c_{2}(n^{-\gamma_{2}}\wedge\|x\|_{H}),

(237)

\displaystyle\|\Pi^{n}(x)\|_{H_{1/2}}\leq c_{2}\|x\|_{H_{1/2}},

(238)

\displaystyle\|(D\Pi^{n})(x)-\operatorname{Id}_{H}\|_{L(H,H)}\leq c_{2}\|x\|_{H},

(239)

\displaystyle\|(-A)^{\gamma_{4}}((D^{n}\Pi)(x)-L^{n}(x))\|_{L(H,H)}\leq c_{2}\|x\|_{H_{\gamma_{4}}},

(240)

\displaystyle\|(D\Pi^{n})(x)(Ax)-A\Pi^{n}(x)\|_{H}\leq c_{2}n^{-\gamma_{3}}\|x\|_{H_{1/2}}(\|x\|_{H_{1/2}}+1)(\|x\|_{H}+1)^{c_{3}},

(241)

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum^{\infty}_{i=1}(D^{2}\Pi^{n})(x)\big(ze_{i},ze_{i}\big)\Big\|_{H}\leq c_{2}\|x\|_{H}\cdot\|z\|^{2}_{HS(H,H)}.

(242)

Then it holds that

\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\sup_{n\in\mathbbm{N}}\|Y^{N,n}_{t}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{1/2})}<\infty,

(243)

that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\sup_{n\in\mathbbm{N}\cap[T,\infty)}n^{\gamma_{3}\wedge\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\bigg\|\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\Big(F_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})+(D\Pi^{n})(X^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n},t})(A_{N}X^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n},t})\\
&\qquad-A_{N}\Pi^{n}(X^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n},t})+\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{J}}(D^{2}\Pi^{n})(X^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n},t})\Big(B_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\tilde{e}_{i},B_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\tilde{e}_{i}\Big)\\
&\qquad-F_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{t})\Big)\bigg\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{-1/2})}<\infty,\end{split}

(244)

that

\begin{split}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\sup_{n\in\mathbbm{N}\cap[T,\infty)}&n^{\gamma_{3}\wedge\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}\Big\|\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\Big((D\Pi^{n})(X^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n},t})(B_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}}))\\
&\qquad-B_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{t})\Big)\Big\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};HS(H,H))}<\infty,\end{split}

(245)

and that

\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\sup_{n\in\mathbbm{N}\cap[T,\infty)}\mathbbm{E}\Big[\exp\Big(V(t,Y^{N,n}_{t})+\int_{0}^{t}\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner s\lrcorner_{n}})\overline{V}(Y^{N,n}_{s})\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\Big]<\infty.

(246)

Proof.

We prove the assertion by an application of Proposition

3.8
.
Therefore, we will check all of the assumptions.
First note that for all
t\in[0,T]
,
s\in[t,T]
, and all
x\in H

it holds that

V(r,e^{tA}x)=e^{-2\eta^{2}r}(\|e^{tA}x\|^{2}_{H}+1)\leq e^{-2\eta^{2}(r-t)}(\|x\|^{2}_{H}+1)=V(r-t,x),

(247)

and this verifies (
87
).
Moreover,
Lemma 4.8 in Jentzen, Lindner & Pus̆nik

[
36
]
,
Lemma 4.3(v) in Jentzen, Lindner & Pus̆nik

[
36
]
, and
(
223
)
prove that there exists a
C\in(0,\infty)
such that
for all
N\in\mathbbm{N}
,

n\in\mathbbm{N}
,
and all
x,y\in H
it holds that

\begin{split}&\|F_{N}(x)-F_{N}(y)\|_{H_{-1/2}}=\|P_{N}(F(P_{N}x)-F(P_{N}y))\|_{H_{-1/2}}\\
\leq{}&|c_{1}|\|(P_{N}x)^{2}-(P_{N}y)^{2}\|_{H}\leq{}|c_{1}|\|P_{N}(x-y)\|_{H}\|P_{N}(x+y)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,1),\mathbbm{R})}\\
\leq{}&C\|x-y\|_{H}\|x+y\|_{H_{1/2}}\end{split}

(248)

and this assures (
85
)
(with
\alpha\curvearrowleft-\tfrac{1}{2}
,
\beta\curvearrowleft 0
,

\gamma\curvearrowleft\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}
,

c_{8}\curvearrowleft 1
).
In addition, the fact that
\inf_{n\in\mathbbm{N}}|\lambda_{n}|\geq 1
,
Lemma 4.13(ii) in Jentzen, Lindner & Pus̆nik

[
36
]
(with
y\curvearrowleft 0
,
c_{0}\curvearrowleft 1
)
and (
223
)
show for all
N\in\mathbbm{N}
,

n\in\mathbbm{N}
,
and all
x\in D_{n}
that

\|F_{N}(x)\|_{H_{-1/2}}\leq\|F_{N}(x)\|_{H}=\|P_{N}F(P_{N}x)\|_{H}\leq{}\tfrac{|c_{1}|}{\sqrt{3}}\|P_{N}x\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}\leq{}\tfrac{|c_{1}|}{\sqrt{3}}T^{\gamma_{1}}n^{-\gamma_{1}},

(249)

which verifies (
89
).
Furthermore, (
223
) imply for all

n\in\mathbbm{N}

and all
(t,x)\in[0,T]\times D_{n}
that

\begin{split}&e^{2\eta^{2}t}\Big((\tfrac{\partial}{\partial t}V)(t,x)+\langle\nabla_{x}V(t,x),F_{N}(x)+A_{N}(x)\rangle_{H}+\tfrac{1}{2}\langle B_{N}(x),(\operatorname{Hess}_{x}V)(t,x)B_{N}(x)\rangle_{HS(H,H)}\\

(260)

In addition, the fact that for all

proves for all

,

, and all
x,y\in H
that

\begin{split}&|V(t,x)-V(s,y)|={}\big|e^{-t\eta^{2}}(\|x\|^{2}_{H}+1)-e^{-s\eta^{2}}(\|y\|^{2}_{H}+1)\big|\\
={}&\big|e^{-t\eta^{2}}(1-e^{(t-s)\eta^{2}})(\|x\|_{H}^{2}+1)+\langle x-y,x+y\rangle_{H}e^{-s\eta^{2}}\big|\\
\leq{}&V(t,x)(s-t)\eta^{2}+\|x-y\|_{H}(\|y-x+2x\|_{H}e^{-s\eta^{2}})\\
\leq{}&(s-t+\|x-y\|_{H})(V(t,x)\eta^{2}+\|y-x+2x\|_{H}e^{-s\eta^{2}})\\
\leq{}&(s-t+\|x-y\|_{H})\big(V(t,x)\eta^{2}+\|y-x\|_{H}+\|x\|^{2}_{H}+1\big)\\
\leq{}&(s-t+\|x-y\|_{H})(V(t,x)\eta^{2}+\|y-x\|_{H}+V(t,x)e^{t\eta^{2}}+1)\\
\leq{}&(s-t+\|x-y\|_{H})(V(t,x)(\eta^{2}+e^{T\eta^{2}})+\|y-x\|_{H}+1)\\
\leq{}&(s-t+\|x-y\|_{H})(\eta^{2}+e^{T\eta^{2}})(V(t,x)+\|y-x\|_{H}+1)\end{split}

(262)

and this shows (
102
).

3.8

The next proposition shows that tamed exponential Euler approximations
of stochastic Burgers equations converge with strong temporal convergence rate
1/2
.

Proposition 5.5
.

Assume Setting
5.1
,

c_{3}\in[1,\infty)
,
let
p\in[4,\infty)
,

\gamma_{2}\in[-\gamma_{1}-\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}},\gamma_{1}+\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}]

\gamma_{3}\in[0,\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}]
,

\gamma_{4}\in(0,\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}})

let
X^{N}\colon[0,T]\times\Omega\to H
,

,
be adapted stochastic processes with continuous sample paths
satisfying for all
N\in\mathbbm{N}

and all
t\in[0,T]
a.s. that

X_{t}^{N}={}e^{tA_{N}}P_{N}(X^{N}_{0})+\int^{t}_{0}e^{(t-s)A_{N}}F_{N}(X^{N}_{s})\,\mathrm{d}s+\int^{t}_{0}e^{(t-s)A_{N}}B_{N}(X^{N}_{s})\,\mathrm{d}W_{s},

(266)

assume for all
x\in H
,
z\in HS(H,H)
and all
n\in\mathbbm{N}
that

\displaystyle\|\Pi^{n}(x)\|_{H}\leq c_{2}(n^{-\gamma_{2}}\wedge\|x\|_{H}),

(267)

\displaystyle\|\Pi^{n}(x)\|_{H_{1/2}}\leq c_{2}\|x\|_{H_{1/2}},

(268)

\displaystyle\|(D\Pi^{n})(x)-\operatorname{Id}_{H}\|_{L(H,H)}\leq c_{2}\|x\|_{H},

(269)

\displaystyle\|(-A)^{\gamma_{4}}((D\Pi)(x)-L(x))\|_{L(H,H)}\leq c_{2}\|x\|_{H_{\gamma_{4}}},

(270)

(271)

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{i\in\mathbbm{N}}(D^{2}\Pi^{n})(x)\big(ze_{i},ze_{i}\big)\Big\|_{H}\leq c_{2}\|x\|_{H}\cdot\|z\|^{2}_{HS(H,H)}.

(272)

Then for all
r\in(0,p)

there exists a
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all

,

(273)

Proof.

First note that existence of
X^{N}
,

[

]
.

Z^{N,n}_{s,t}=\int_{s}^{t}e^{(t-u)A}B_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner u\lrcorner_{n}})\,\mathrm{d}W_{u},

(274)

denote by

,

\displaystyle\begin{split}a^{N,n}_{t}={}&A_{N}Y^{N,n}_{t}+\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\Big(F_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})+(D\Pi^{n})(Z^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n},t})(A_{N}Z^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n},t})-A_{N}\Pi^{n}(Z^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n},t})\\
&+\tfrac{1}{2}\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\sum_{i=1}^{\infty}(D^{2}\Pi^{n})(Z^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n},t})\Big(B_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})e_{i},B_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})e_{i}\Big)\Big),\end{split}

(275)

denote by

(276)

\begin{split}&\exp\Big(\int_{0}^{\tau_{N,n}\wedge t}\Big[\frac{\langle X^{N}_{s}-Y^{N,n}_{s},A_{N}X^{N}_{s}+F_{N}(X^{N}_{s})-\tfrac{1}{2}A_{N}(X^{N}_{s}+Y^{N,n}_{s})-F_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{s})\rangle_{H}}{\|X^{N}_{s}-Y^{N,n}_{s}\|^{2}_{H}}\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{(p-1)\|B_{N}(X^{N}_{s})-B_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{s})\|^{2}_{HS(H,H)}}{\|X^{N}_{s}-Y^{N,n}_{s}\|^{2}_{H}}+2\tfrac{p-1}{p}\Big]^{+}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\\
={}&\exp\Big(\tfrac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{\tau_{N,n}\wedge t}\Big[\frac{\langle X^{N}_{s}-Y^{N,n}_{s},A_{N}X^{N}_{s}+2F_{N}(X^{N}_{s})-2F_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{s})-A_{N}Y^{N,n}_{s}\rangle_{H}}{\|X^{N}_{s}-Y^{N,n}_{s}\|^{2}_{H}}\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{2(p-1)\|B_{N}(X^{N}_{s})-B_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{s})\|^{2}_{HS(H,H)}}{\|X^{N}_{s}-Y^{N,n}_{s}\|^{2}_{H}}+4\tfrac{p-1}{p}\Big]^{+}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\\
\leq{}&\exp\Big(\int_{0}^{T}\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner s\lrcorner_{n}})\big(e^{-2\eta^{2}T}\tfrac{{\varepsilon}}{q}\,(\|X_{s}^{N}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}+\|Y_{s}^{N,n}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}})+C\big)\,\mathrm{d}s\Big).\end{split}

(284)

Combining this with (
280
)
and (
281
)
shows that for all
q\in(0,\infty)

there exists a
C\in(0,\infty)

such that for all
N\in\mathbbm{N}
,
n\in\mathbbm{N}\cap[T,\infty)
and all
t\in[0,T]

it holds that

\begin{split}&\bigg\|\exp\Big(\int_{0}^{\tau_{N,n}\wedge t}\Big[\frac{\langle X^{N}_{s}-Y^{N,n}_{s},A_{N}X^{N}_{s}+F_{N}(X^{N}_{s})-\tfrac{1}{2}A_{N}(X^{N}_{s}+Y^{N,n}_{s})-F_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{s})\rangle_{H}}{\|X^{N}_{s}-Y^{N,n}_{s}\|^{2}_{H}}\\
&\qquad\qquad+\frac{(p-1)\|B_{N}(X^{N}_{s})-B_{N}(Y^{N,n}_{s})\|^{2}_{HS(H,H)}}{\|X^{N}_{s}-Y^{N,n}_{s}\|^{2}_{H}}+2\tfrac{p-1}{p}\Big]^{+}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big)\bigg\|_{L^{q}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\\
\leq{}&C.\end{split}

(285)

Moreover,
Young’s inequality
yields
for all

N,n\in\mathbbm{N}
and all
t\in[0,T]

that

\begin{split}&\mathbbm{E}\Big[\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\cdot\Big(\|X_{t}^{N}-Y_{t}^{N,n}\|^{(p-2)}_{H}\\
&\qquad\big[\langle X_{t}^{N}-Y_{t}^{N,n},\tfrac{1}{2}A_{N}(X_{t}^{N}+Y_{t}^{N,n})+F_{N}(Y_{t}^{N,n})-a^{N,n}_{t}\rangle_{H}\\
&\qquad+(p-1)\|b^{N,n}_{t}-B_{N}(Y_{t}^{N,n})\|^{2}_{HS(H,H)}-2\tfrac{p-2}{p}\|X_{t}^{N}-Y_{t}^{N,n}\|^{2}_{H}\big]\Big)^{+}\Big]\\
\leq{}&\Big\|\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\cdot\Big(2\tfrac{p-2}{p}\|X_{t}^{N}-Y_{t}^{N,n}\|^{p}_{H}\\
&\qquad+\tfrac{2}{p}\big[\big|(\langle X_{t}^{N}-Y_{t}^{N,n},A_{N}Y_{t}^{N,n}+F_{N}(Y_{t}^{N,n})-a^{N,n}_{t}\rangle_{H}-\tfrac{1}{2}\|X_{t}^{N}-Y_{t}^{N,n}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}})^{+}\big|^{p/2}\\
&\qquad+(p-1)^{p/2}\|b^{N,n}_{t}-B_{N}(Y_{t}^{N,n})\|^{p}_{HS(H,H)}\big]-2\tfrac{p-2}{p}\|X_{t}^{N}-Y_{t}^{N,n}\|^{p}_{H}\Big)^{+}\Big\|_{L^{1}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\\
\leq{}&\tfrac{2}{p}\big\|\big(\|X_{t}^{N}-Y_{t}^{N,n}\|_{H_{1/2}}\|\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\big(A_{N}Y_{t}^{N,n}+F_{N}(Y_{t}^{N,n})-a^{N,n}_{t}\big)\|_{H_{-1/2}}\\
&\qquad-\tfrac{1}{2}\|X_{t}^{N}-Y_{t}^{N,n}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}\big)^{+}\big\|^{\nicefrac{{p}}{{2}}}_{L^{p/2}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\\
&\qquad+\tfrac{2(p-1)^{p/2}}{p}\|\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\big(b^{N,n}_{t}-B_{N}(Y_{t}^{N,n})\big)\|^{p}_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};HS(H,H))}.\end{split}

(286)

Furthermore,
Young’s inequality,
(
282
) and
(
283
)
establish
that
there exists a
C\in(0,\infty)

such that
for all

N,n\in\mathbbm{N}

it holds
that

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{T}\tfrac{2}{p}\big\|\big(\|X_{t}^{N}-Y_{t}^{N,n}\|_{H_{1/2}}\|\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\big(A_{N}Y_{t}^{N,n}+F_{N}(Y_{t}^{N,n})-a^{N,n}_{t}\big)\|_{H_{-1/2}}\\
&\qquad-\tfrac{1}{2}\|X_{t}^{N}-Y_{t}^{N,n}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}\big)^{+}\big\|^{\nicefrac{{p}}{{2}}}_{L^{p/2}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\\
&\qquad+\tfrac{2(p-1)^{p/2}}{p}\|\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\big(b^{N,n}_{t}-B_{N}(Y_{t}^{N,n})\big)\|^{p}_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};HS(H,H))}\,\mathrm{d}t\\
\leq{}&\int_{0}^{T}\tfrac{2}{p}\big\|\tfrac{1}{2}\|\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\big(A_{N}Y_{t}^{N,n}+F_{N}(Y_{t}^{N,n})-a^{N,n}_{t}\big)\|^{2}_{H_{-1/2}}\big\|^{\nicefrac{{p}}{{2}}}_{L^{p/2}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\\
&\qquad+\tfrac{2(p-1)^{p/2}}{p}\|\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\big(b^{N,n}_{t}-B_{N}(Y_{t}^{N,n})\big)\|^{p}_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};HS(H,H))}\,\mathrm{d}t\\
={}&\int_{0}^{T}\tfrac{1}{p}\big\|\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\big(A_{N}Y_{t}^{N,n}+F_{N}(Y_{t}^{N,n})-a^{N,n}_{t}\big)\big\|^{p}_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H_{-1/2})}\\
&\qquad+\tfrac{2(p-1)^{p/2}}{p}\|\mathbbm{1}_{D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\llcorner t\lrcorner_{n}})\big(b^{N,n}_{t}-B_{N}(Y_{t}^{N,n})\big)\|^{p}_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};HS(H,H))}\,\mathrm{d}t\\
\leq{}&\int_{0}^{T}Cn^{-\gamma_{3}p}+Cn^{-\gamma_{3}p}\,\mathrm{d}t\leq{}2TCn^{-\gamma_{3}p}.\end{split}

(287)

Thus, Lemma
4.1

(with
{\varepsilon}\curvearrowleft 1
,
\mu\curvearrowleft A_{N}+F_{N}
,
\sigma\curvearrowleft B_{N}
,

\psi\curvearrowleft(H^{2}\ni(x,y)\mapsto\tfrac{1}{2}A_{N}(x+y)+F_{N}(y)\in H)
,

\varphi\curvearrowleft(H^{2}\ni(x,y)\mapsto B_{N}(y)\in HS(H,H))
,

\tau\curvearrowleft\tau_{N,n}\wedge t

and
\chi\curvearrowleft 2\tfrac{p-1}{p}
),
(
285
),
(
286
), and
(
287
)
imply that for all
r\in(0,p)
there exists a

C\in(0,\infty)

such that for all
N\in\mathbbm{N}
,
n\in\mathbbm{N}\cap[T,\infty)

and all
t\in[0,T]

it holds that

\|X_{\tau_{N,n}\wedge t}^{N}-Y_{\tau_{N,n}\wedge t}^{N,n}\|_{L^{r}(\mathbbm{P};H)}\leq C\|X^{N}_{0}-Y^{N,n}_{0}\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H)}+Cn^{-\gamma_{3}}.

(288)

Next note that Proposition
5.4
(with
p\curvearrowleft q
)
demonstrates for all
q\in[2,\infty]
that

\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\sup_{n\in\mathbbm{N}}\|Y^{N,n}_{t}\|_{L^{q}(\mathbbm{P};H)}\leq\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\sup_{n\in\mathbbm{N}}\|Y^{N,n}_{t}\|_{L^{q}(\mathbbm{P};H_{1/2})}<\infty.

(289)

Moreover, (
281
)
ensures for all
q\in[4,\infty]
that

\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\|X^{N}_{t}\|_{L^{q}(\mathbbm{P};H)}<\infty.

(290)

In addition, (
277
) implies for all
N\in\mathbbm{N}

and all
n\in\mathbbm{N}\cap[T,\infty)
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\mathbbm{1}_{H\backslash D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\tau_{N,n}})\|^{2p}_{L^{2p}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}=\mathbbm{P}\big(\|Y^{N,n}_{\tau_{N,n}}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}>\big(\tfrac{T}{n}\big)^{\gamma_{1}}\big)\\
\leq{}&\mathbbm{P}\big(\cup^{n}_{s=0}\big\{\|Y^{N,n}_{sT/n}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}>\big(\tfrac{T}{n}\big)^{\gamma_{1}}\big\}\big)\leq{}\sum_{s\in\{0,\frac{T}{n},\ldots,T\}}\mathbbm{P}\big(\|Y^{N,n}_{s}\|^{2}_{H_{1/2}}>\big(\tfrac{T}{n}\big)^{\gamma_{1}}\big)\\
\leq{}&(n+1)\sup_{s\in[0,T]}\frac{\mathbbm{E}[\|Y^{N,n}_{s}\|_{H_{1/2}}^{-2(p+1)/\gamma_{1}}]\,n^{-(p+1)}}{T^{-(p+1)}}\leq{}\sup_{s\in[0,T]}\frac{2\mathbbm{E}[\|Y^{N,n}_{s}\|_{H_{1/2}}^{-2(p+1)/\gamma_{1}}]\,n^{-p}}{T^{-(p+1)}}.\end{split}

(291)

Therefore, (
290
) and (
291
)
yield that
there exists a
C\in(0,\infty)
such that for all
n\in\mathbbm{N}\cap[T,\infty)

it holds that

\begin{split}&\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\|(X^{N}_{t}-Y^{N,n}_{t})\mathbbm{1}_{H\backslash D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\tau_{N,n}})\|_{L^{p}(\mathbbm{P};H)}\\
\leq{}&\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\Big((\|X^{N}_{t}\|_{L^{2p}(\mathbbm{P};H)}+\|Y^{N,n}_{t}\|_{L^{2p}(\mathbbm{P};H)})\|\mathbbm{1}_{H\backslash D_{n}}(Y^{N,n}_{\tau_{N,n}})\|_{L^{2p}(\mathbbm{P};\mathbbm{R})}\Big)\\
\leq{}&2C\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\sup_{s\in[0,T]}\bigg(\bigg(\frac{2\mathbbm{E}[\|Y^{N,n}_{s}\|_{H_{1/2}}^{-2(p+1)/\gamma_{1}}]\,n^{-p}}{T^{-(p+1)}}\bigg)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2p}}}\bigg)\leq{}4C^{2}T^{(p+1)/2p}n^{-\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}.\end{split}

(292)

Combining (
279
), (
288
)
and (
292
)
completes the proof of Proposition
5.5
.
∎

In the next lemma we give sufficient conditions on the taming function
\Pi
.

Lemma 5.6
.

Let

(H,\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{H},\|\cdot\|_{H})
and

(U,\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{U},\|\cdot\|_{U})

be separable
\mathbbm{R}
-Hilbert spaces with
\#H\wedge\#U>1
,
let
\mathcal{J}\subseteq\mathbbm{N}
,
let

(\tilde{e}_{j})_{j\in\mathcal{J}}\subseteq U
, be an orthonormal basis of
U
,
let
f\in C^{2}([0,\infty),\mathbbm{R})

satisfy that

f(0)=1
,

\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|(t\vee 1)f(t^{2})|<\infty
,

\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|(t\vee 1)f^{\prime}(t^{2})|<\infty
,

\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|tf^{\prime\prime}(t)|<\infty,

let
R,\gamma\in[0,\infty)
,

C\in(0,\infty)
,

\gamma_{1}\in[0,\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}})
,
let
\Pi^{n}\colon H\to H
,
n\in\mathbbm{N}
,
satisfy for all
x\in H
and all
n\in\mathbbm{N}
that

\Pi^{n}(x)={}f(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})\,x,

(293)

and let
L^{n}\colon H\to L(H,H)
,
n\in\mathbbm{N}
,
satisfy for all
x,y\in H
and all
n\in\mathbbm{N}
that

L^{n}(x)y={}f(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})\,y.

(294)

Then there exists a
c\in(0,\infty)

such that for all
x\in H
,
z\in HS(U,H)
,
n\in\mathbbm{N}
,

r\in[0,R]
,
and all
A\in L(H,H)
with
\inf_{x\in H\backslash\{0\}}\tfrac{\|Ax\|_{H}}{\|x\|_{H}}\geq C

and with
\forall x\in H\colon\langle Ax,x\rangle_{H}=\langle x,Ax\rangle_{H}<0

it holds that

\displaystyle\Pi^{n}\in\operatorname{C}^{2}(H,H),

(295)

\displaystyle\|\Pi^{n}(x)\|_{H}\leq c\,n^{\gamma},

(296)

\displaystyle\|(-A)^{r}\Pi^{n}(x)\|_{H}\leq c\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H},

(297)

\displaystyle\|(-A)^{r}((D\Pi^{n})(x)-\operatorname{Id}_{H})(-A)^{-r}\|_{L(H,H)}\leq c\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H},

(298)

\displaystyle\|(-A)^{\gamma_{1}}((D\Pi^{n})(x)-L^{n}(x))(-A)^{-r}\|_{L(H,H)}\leq c\|(-A)^{r+\gamma_{1}}x\|_{H},

(299)

\displaystyle\big\|(-A)^{r}\big((D\Pi^{n})(x)(Ax)-A\Pi^{n}(x)\big)\big\|_{H}\leq c\,n^{-2\gamma}\|(-A)^{r+\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}x\|_{H}(\|(-A)^{r+\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}x\|_{H}+1)(\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}+1),

(300)

\displaystyle\Big\|(-A)^{r}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{J}}(D^{2}\Pi^{n})(x)\big(z\tilde{e}_{i},z\tilde{e}_{i}\big)\Big\|_{H}\leq c\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}\cdot\|(-A)^{r}z\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}.

(301)

Proof.

First note that (
293
)
and
f\in\operatorname{C}^{2}([0,\infty),\mathbbm{R})
imply
for all
n\in\mathbbm{N}
and all
x,y,z\in H
that
\Pi^{n}\in\operatorname{C}^{2}(H,H)

and that

\displaystyle(D\Pi^{n})(x)(y)={}f(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})y+n^{-2\gamma}x\langle x,y\rangle_{H}f^{\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma}),

(302)

\displaystyle\begin{split}&(D^{2}\Pi^{n})(x)(y,z)\\
={}&n^{-2\gamma}y\langle x,z\rangle_{H}f^{\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})+n^{-2\gamma}z\langle x,y\rangle_{H}f^{\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})\\
&+n^{-2\gamma}x\langle z,y\rangle_{H}f^{\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})+n^{-4\gamma}x\langle x,y\rangle_{H}\langle x,z\rangle_{H}f^{\prime\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})\\
={}&n^{-2\gamma}f^{\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})\big(y\langle x,z\rangle_{H}+z\langle x,y\rangle_{H}+x\langle y,z\rangle_{H}\big)\\
&+n^{-4\gamma}x\langle x,y\rangle_{H}\langle x,z\rangle_{H}f^{\prime\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma}).\end{split}

(303)

Next note, that (
293
)
implies
for all
x\in H
that

\displaystyle\|\Pi^{n}(x)\|_{H}=|f(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})|(\|x\|_{H}^{2}n^{-2\gamma})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}n^{\gamma}\leq 2n^{\gamma}\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|t\cdot f(t^{2})|

(304)

and that for all
r\in[0,R]

and all
A\in L(H,H)
with
\inf_{x\in H\backslash\{0\}}\tfrac{\|Ax\|_{H}}{\|x\|_{H}}\geq C

and with
\forall x\in H\colon\langle Ax,x\rangle_{H}=\langle x,Ax\rangle_{H}<0

it holds that

\displaystyle\|(-A)^{r}\Pi^{n}(x)\|_{H}=f(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}\leq\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|f(t)|.

(305)

Moreover, the assumption
f(0)=1
implies that

\lim_{x\to 0}\frac{f(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})-1}{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma}}=f^{\prime}(0).

(306)

Thus,
there
exists an
{\varepsilon}\in(0,1]
, which we fix for the rest of the proof, such that
for all
x\in H
with
\|x\|_{H}\leq{\varepsilon}
it holds that

\displaystyle|f(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})-1|\leq\big(|f^{\prime}(0)|+|f^{\prime}(0)|+2)\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma}\leq(|f^{\prime}(0)|+1)\|x\|_{H}.

(307)

In addition, for all
x\in H
with
\|x\|_{H}\geq{\varepsilon}
it holds that

\displaystyle|f(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})-1|\leq 1+\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|f(t)|\leq{\varepsilon}^{-1}(1+\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|f(t)|)\|x\|_{H}.

(308)

Combining (
302
), (
307
), and (
309
)
shows
for all
x,y\in H
,
r\in[0,R]
,
and all
A\in L(H,H)
with
\inf_{x\in H\backslash\{0\}}\tfrac{\|Ax\|_{H}}{\|x\|_{H}}\geq C

and with
\forall x\in H\colon\langle Ax,x\rangle_{H}=\langle x,Ax\rangle_{H}<0

that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|(-A)^{r}((D\Pi^{n})(x)(y)-y)\|_{H}\\
\leq{}&|f(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})-1|\cdot\|(-A)^{r}y\|_{H}+n^{-2\gamma}\|x\|_{H}\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}\|y\|_{H}|f^{\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})|\\
\leq{}&\Big({\varepsilon}^{-1}(1+\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|f(t)|)+|f^{\prime}(0)|+1\Big)\|x\|_{H}\cdot\|(-A)^{r}y\|_{H}\\
&+(n^{-2\gamma}\|x\|_{H}^{2})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}n^{-\gamma}\|y\|_{H}\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}|f^{\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})|\\
\leq{}&\Big({\varepsilon}^{-1}(1+\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|f(t)|)+|f^{\prime}(0)|+1+2\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|tf^{\prime}(t^{2})|\Big)C^{-r}\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}\cdot\|(-A)^{r}y\|_{H}\\
\leq{}&\Big({\varepsilon}^{-1}(1+\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|f(t)|)+|f^{\prime}(0)|+1+2\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|tf^{\prime}(t^{2})|\Big)(1\vee C^{-R})\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}\cdot\|(-A)^{r}y\|_{H}.\end{split}

(309)

Analogously it follows for all
x,y\in H
,
r\in[0,R]
,
and all
A\in L(H,H)
with
\inf_{x\in H\backslash\{0\}}\tfrac{\|Ax\|_{H}}{\|x\|_{H}}\geq C

and with
\forall x\in H\colon\langle Ax,x\rangle_{H}=\langle x,Ax\rangle_{H}<0
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|(-A)^{r+\gamma_{1}}((D\Pi^{n})(x)(y)-L(x)y)\|_{H}\\
={}&n^{-2\gamma}\|(-A)^{r+\gamma_{1}}x\|_{H}\langle x,y\rangle_{H}f^{\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})\\
\leq{}&(n^{-2\gamma}\|x\|_{H}^{2})^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}n^{-\gamma}\|y\|_{H}\|(-A)^{r+\gamma_{1}}x\|_{H}|f^{\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})|\\
\leq{}&2\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|tf^{\prime}(t^{2})|\cdot\|(-A)^{r+\gamma_{1}}x\|_{H}\cdot\|y\|_{H}.\end{split}

(310)

Next note, that (
302
) verifies for all
x\in H
,
r\in[0,R]
,
and all
A\in L(H,H)
with
\inf_{x\in H\backslash\{0\}}\tfrac{\|Ax\|_{H}}{\|x\|_{H}}\geq C

and with
\forall x\in H\colon\langle Ax,x\rangle_{H}=\langle x,Ax\rangle_{H}<0
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|(-A)^{r}((D\Pi^{n})(x)(Ax)-A\Pi^{n}(x))\|_{H}\\
={}&n^{-2\gamma}\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}\cdot\|(-A)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}x\|^{2}_{H}|f^{\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})|\\
\leq{}&n^{-2\gamma}C^{-r}(\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}+1)(\|(-A)^{r+\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}x\|_{H}+1)\|x\|_{H_{1/2+r}}\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|f^{\prime}(t)|\\
\leq{}&n^{-2\gamma}(1\vee C^{-R})(\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}+1)(\|(-A)^{r+\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}x\|_{H}+1)\|x\|_{H_{1/2+r}}\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|f^{\prime}(t)|.\end{split}

(311)

Finally (
303
) yields for all
x\in H
,
z\in HS(U,H)
,
r\in[0,R]
,
and all
A\in L(H,H)
with
\inf_{x\in H\backslash\{0\}}\tfrac{\|Ax\|_{H}}{\|x\|_{H}}\geq C

and with
\forall x\in H\colon\langle Ax,x\rangle_{H}=\langle x,Ax\rangle_{H}<0
that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\Big\|(-A)^{r}\sum_{i\in\mathcal{J}}(D^{2}\Pi^{n})(x)\big(z\tilde{e}_{i},z\tilde{e}_{i}\big)\Big\|_{H}\\
\leq{}&\sum_{i\in\mathcal{J}}\Big(n^{-2\gamma}|f^{\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})|\big(2\|(-A)^{r}z\tilde{e}_{i}\|_{H}\langle x,z\tilde{e}_{i}\rangle_{H}+\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}\langle z\tilde{e}_{i},z\tilde{e}_{i}\rangle_{H}\big)\\
&\quad+n^{-4\gamma}\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}\langle x,z\tilde{e}_{i}\rangle_{H}\langle x,z\tilde{e}_{i}\rangle_{H}|f^{\prime\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})|\Big)\\
\leq{}&\sum_{i\in\mathcal{J}}\Big(3C^{-2r}|f^{\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})|\cdot\|(-A)^{r}z\tilde{e}_{i}\|^{2}_{H}\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}\\
&\quad+C^{-2r}(n^{-2\gamma}\|x\|^{2}_{H})\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}\|(-A)^{r}z\tilde{e}_{i}\|^{2}_{H}|f^{\prime\prime}(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-2\gamma})|\Big)\\
\leq{}&(1\vee C^{-2R})\big(3(\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|f^{\prime}(t)|)+2\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}|tf^{\prime\prime}(t)|\big)\|(-A)^{r}x\|_{H}\|(-A)^{r}z\|^{2}_{HS(U,H)}.\end{split}

(312)

Combining (
304
), (
305
),
(
309
), (
310
),
(
311
), and
(
312
)
finishes the proof of Lemma
5.6
.
∎

In the next corollary we combine Proposition
5.5

and
Corollary
5.3

to establish temporal convergence rate
1/2
and spatial convergence rate
1
.

Corollary 5.7
.

Assume Setting
5.1
,
let
\gamma_{1}=\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}
,
let
\xi\in H_{1/2}
,
let
f\in\operatorname{C}^{2}([0,\infty),\mathbbm{R})

satisfy for all
x\in[0,\infty)
that

f(x)=\tfrac{1}{1+2x}
, let

\Pi^{n}\colon H\to H
,
n\in\mathbbm{N}
,
let
satisfy for all
x\in H
and all
n\in\mathbbm{N}
that

\Pi^{n}(x)={}f(\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}\|x\|^{2}_{H}\cdot n^{-1/2})\,x,

(313)

let
Y^{N,n}_{0}=P_{N}(\xi)
,
N,n\in\mathbbm{N}
,
let
X\colon[0,T]\times\Omega\to D((-A)^{\frac{1}{2}})

be an adapted stochastic process with continuous sample paths such that for all
t\in[0,T]

it holds a.s. that

\begin{split}X_{t}=e^{tA}\xi+\int_{0}^{t}e^{(t-s)A}F(X_{s})\,\mathrm{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}e^{(t-s)A}B(X_{s})\,\mathrm{d}W_{s}.\end{split}

(314)

Then
for all
{\varepsilon},p\in(0,\infty)
there exists
C\in\mathbbm{R}
such that for all
N,n\in\mathbbm{N}
it holds that

\begin{split}\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\Big(\mathbbm{E}\Big[\big\|X_{t}-Y_{t}^{N,n}\big\|^{p}_{H}\Big]\Big)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq C\big(N^{{\varepsilon}-1}+n^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big).\end{split}

(315)

Proof.

First denote by
X^{N}\colon[0,T]\times\Omega\to H
,
N\in\mathbbm{N}
,
adapted stochastic processes with continuous sample paths
satisfying for all
N\in\mathbbm{N}

and all
t\in[0,T]
a.s. that

X_{t}^{N}={}e^{tA_{N}}P_{N}(\xi)+\int^{t}_{0}e^{(t-s)A_{N}}F_{N}(X^{N}_{s})\,\mathrm{d}s+\int^{t}_{0}e^{(t-s)A_{N}}B_{N}(X^{N}_{s})\,\mathrm{d}W_{s}.

(316)

Moreover, note that for all
t\in[0,\infty)
it holds that

\displaystyle f^{\prime}(t)=\tfrac{-2}{(1+2t)^{2}},\qquad f^{\prime\prime}(t)=\tfrac{8}{(1+2t)^{3}},

(317)

and thus it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in(0,\infty)}(|(t\vee 1)f(t^{2})|+|(t\vee 1)f^{\prime}(t^{2})|+|tf^{\prime\prime}(t)|)<\infty.

(318)

Therefore, Proposition
5.5

(with
c_{3}\curvearrowleft 1
,
\gamma_{1}\curvearrowleft\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}
,

\gamma_{2}\curvearrowleft\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}
,

\gamma_{3}\curvearrowleft\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}
,

\gamma_{4}\curvearrowleft\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}
)
and
Lemma
5.6

(with
\gamma\curvearrowleft\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}
,
\gamma_{1}\curvearrowleft\nicefrac{{1}}{{4}}
,

r\curvearrowleft\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}
resp.
r\curvearrowleft 0
)
prove that for all
p\in(0,\infty)
there exists a
C\in(0,\infty)
such that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\sup_{N\in\mathbbm{N}}\Big(\mathbbm{E}\Big[\big\|X^{N}_{t}-Y_{t}^{N,n}\big\|^{p}_{H}\Big]\Big)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq{}Cn^{-\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}.

Hence, Corollary
5.3

ensures that for all
p,{\varepsilon}\in(0,\infty)
there exists a
C\in(0,\infty)
such that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\Big(\mathbbm{E}\Big[\big\|X_{t}-Y_{t}^{N,n}\big\|^{p}_{H}\Big]\Big)^{\frac{1}{p}}\leq{}

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\Big(\mathbbm{E}\Big[\big\|X_{t}-X_{t}^{N}\big\|^{p}_{H}\Big]\Big)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\sup_{t\in[0,T]}\Big(\mathbbm{E}\Big[\big\|X^{N}_{t}-Y_{t}^{N,n}\big\|^{p}_{H}\Big]\Big)^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq{}

\displaystyle C(N^{-1}+n^{-\nicefrac{{1}}{{2}}}).

(319)

This finishes the proof of
Corollary

5.7
.
∎
